Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The ode variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 802 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 746 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 802 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the ode structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 746. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any ode module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 671. The projective resolution has length at most 165 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.
